We Need 6 Icons ( Live | Sat2iptv | Movies | Series | Top Movies | Top Series )

Webservice clarification: How to use This web service is used to activate TV Box, ANDROID,Linux,Windows ...etc
It requires you to clearly identify your device: MAC Address, Serial Number, ChipID , Model Name and Firmware Version.
Set activation useragent
Set player useragent

Note for speedup download process:

We send Content-Encoding: gzip
you must: Accept-Encoding gzip, deflate

Failover host:

when our main host goes down for some reason. you need to use this code:
char host1 = ""
char host2 = ""
char default_host = host1;

if( host1 is down)
default_host = host2
store a value for remembering that host1 is down for 10 days.
}
and use default_host for all requesting process.

XOR Encryption

the protocol is 2 way encryption, you must send XOR data and the server will reply XOR data.

You must send data like this: 
headers = Content-Type: multipart/form-data
headers = User-Agent: REV ModelName Firmware Version
    example: User-Agent: REV 2000plus 2.1.1 (this is a sample only)
	
data_xor = xor('{"mode":"active","code":"111111111111","mac":"00:17:B3:EB:A0:59","sn":"0017.B3EB.A059","chipid":"123","model":"2000plus","firmware_ver":"1.2"}');

data_xor = base64_encode(data_xor); //encode the xor data with base64

POST to API: curl_easy_setopt(curl, CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS, "json=data_xor");

json will hold the XOR data

we read data from our server using PHP like this: $json = $_POST['json']
The reply from server is Encrypted using XOR key = 7wEp.20A8opr+3EM]xe.@y](
and is: Content-Transfer-Encoding: binary
You shoud store this key securely inside your code. don't make it exposed to everyone.
And the Result will be JSON code (UTF-8).
	// you can use this function for XOR
	void encryptDecrypt(char *input, char *output){
		
		char MyXorKey[]= {"7wEp.20A8opr+3EM]xe.@y]("};
		int MyKeyLength = strlen(MyXorKey);
		int i = 0;
		for(i=0;i< strlen(input);i++)
		{
			output[i]=input[i]^MyXorKey[i%MyKeyLength];
		}	
	}

Step 1 : Activate

API MAIN Server 
POST http://gean4563t.xyz:80/V6/API-V6.php

json is a variable will hold Encrypted data using XOR key and post to server: 

json: {"mode":"active","code":"111111111111","mac":"00:17:B3:EB:A0:59","sn":"0017.B3EB.A059","chipid":"123","model":"2000plus","firmware_ver":"1.2"}

Parameters:
------------
  • mode => active (call active code function)
  • code => ActiveCode (user input it)
  • mac => MAC Address
  • sn => Serial Number
  • chipid => chipid of stb box , if you don't have it: leave it blank.
  • model => stb model name
  • firmware_ver => stb firmware version
  • osd_test => osd_test is used from your side to test On Screen Message.
    json: {"osd_test":"Hi this is test osd","mode":"active","code":"111111111111","mac":"00:17:B3:EB:A0:59","sn":"0017.B3EB.A059","chipid":"123","model":"2000plus","firmware_ver":"1.2"}
    when you send osd_test, you will see it on JSON reply.
You Need to call this function once. This will create a user account for device
and activate it
This function must be called only once for first use.
Further calling of this function will not do much more.

Returned Value: JSON 
{
    "status": 100,
    "message": "The Code is active",
    "osd": "this is osd message test. you can test it yourself by passing : osd_test. ",
    "expire": "538 Days",
    "user_agent": "",
    "code_id": "1642"
}

before start coding and querying http server please do the following checks.
1- check the reply from server, if it's a valid JSON format, then it's okay else print message for the user like this: (Server is down, please try again later.)
2- when requesting http, give the server time to reply: set_time_out to maximum eg. 30 seconds timewait.

  • Status =
    100 = The Code is active for the first time./ The Code is active.
    102 = The Code is Suspended.
    103 = The Code is Not Found.
    104 = The Code is expired.
  • message => the reply message from server 
    	// this code is used to check osd has value or not.
	if(status == 100 ){
		//if the status = 100 , then it's success, procced to next step. 
		your code for call process  to next step here;
	
	}
	else{
	    // if status not equal 100, then print the message from server.
		// pop a message on screen so the user see it.
	    print( message );
	}
  • osd => On Screen Message. Example: Hello User, Please update Your Software Now. (if the osd is not empty you must display this osd on screen.)
    you need to check OSD like this: 
    // you must write code like this:
	// you need to check if osd has value and not empty.
	if(osd != "" ){
		//if the osd var is not empty, then pop a message on screen 
		print osd;
		// message will show for only  seconds. then it will disappear.
	}
  • expire => the expiration date of account

  • user_agent => is the User-agent in mediaplayer. is used to play streams.

  • message + "expire":"538 Days" must be popup to user (OSD Message), so he can know the expiration date.
SAT 2 IPTV

Normal SAT Channel to IPTV

Note: the code must be activated first to Access this SAT 2 IPTV.

This feature allow user to watch iptv channels form Normal Transponder channels list.
When user NOT on iptv (when he watch normal channels) and when he hit on scrambled channel name
you must compare with JOSN data and if match go and play the match iptv channel.

When sat2iptv server is down, make sure to ignore device reboot and freez.
if server is not readable, you should ignore it.
please add all needed checks to avoid Freez/Reboot. 

http://gean4563t.xyz:80/V6/API-V6.php
json: {"mode":"sat2iptv","code":"111111111111","mac":"00:17:B3:EB:A0:59","sn":"0017.B3EB.A059","chipid":"123","model":"2000plus","firmware_ver":"1.2"}

Parameters:

  • mode = sat2iptv (will list all available SAT to IPTV channels.)
  • code , mac , sn, chipid,model, firmware_ver: already mentioned.

Returned Value: JSON

[
    {
        "channel_name": "beIN Sports 1 HD",
        "angle": "",
        "tp": null,
        "pol": "",
        "sid": null,
        "stream_url": "http://gean4563t.xyz:80/mac_1642/284989241627950540f22f/86607"
    },
    {
        "channel_name": "beIN Sports 2 HD",
        "angle": "",
        "tp": null,
        "pol": "",
        "sid": null,
        "stream_url": "http://gean4563t.xyz:80/mac_1642/284989241627950540f22f/133251"
    },
    {
        "channel_name": "beIN Sports 3 HD",
        "angle": "",
        "tp": null,
        "pol": "",
        "sid": null,
        "stream_url": "http://gean4563t.xyz:80/mac_1642/284989241627950540f22f/77179"

    {
        "channel_name": "beIN Sports Premium1 HD",
        "angle": "",
        "tp": null,
        "pol": "",
        "sid": null,
        "stream_url": "http://gean4563t.xyz:80/mac_1642/284989241627950540f22f/86613"
    }
]
  • channel_name => Channel Name.
  • angle => Satellite Angel.
  • tp => Transponder
  • pol => Polarization (H/V).
  • sid => SID Service ID.
  • stream_url => take this url and pass it to the video player (if IPTV match).

Fix for Low CPU & RAM

Lite Packages & Channels for low Quality STB with low CPU and low RAM.

the lite_packages and lite_channels are used for low cpu and low ram STB.
first you need to call: lite_packages
second: when user choose any package form packages list , then you need to do another call lite_channels with pkg_id.
each time the user chooses the package, then you go and download channels list from: lite_channels
this method will not download all channels, it will download only the chosen ones. 
http://gean4563t.xyz:80/V6/API-V6.php
json: {"mode":"lite_packages","code":"111111111111","mac":"00:17:B3:EB:A0:59","sn":"0017.B3EB.A059","chipid":"123","model":"2000plus","firmware_ver":"1.2"}

Parameters:

  • mode = lite_packages (call packages function)
  • code , mac , sn , model, firmware_ver: already mentioned.

Returned value: JSON Data

VOD | Movies

Step 1 : Movies Categories

Get Categories Menu with sub categories.
json: {"mode":"movies_cat","code":"111111111111","mac":"00:17:B3:EB:A0:59","sn":"0017.B3EB.A059","chipid":"123","model":"2000plus","firmware_ver":"1.2"}

Parameters:

  • mode = movies_cat (will list all available Movies Categories with sub child categories.)
  • code , mac , sn, chipid, model, firmware_ver: already mentioned.

  • cat_id => parent category ID
  • cat_name => parent category name
  • cat_icon => parent category image.
  • cat_order => used to list category in Sortable manner.
  • sub_cats => the sub categories.
    • sub_id => sub category image.
    • sub_name => sub category image.
    • sub_icon => sub category image.
    • sub_view_order => used to list sub category in Sortable manner.

Step 2 : Movies List

List of Movies. (you must pass catid to API inorder to get Movies list.)
use the (sub_id) value for getting movies list. 
http://gean4563t.xyz:80/V6/API-V6.php
json: {"mode":"movies_list","code":"111111111111","mac":"00:17:B3:EB:A0:59","sn":"0017.B3EB.A059","chipid":"123","model":"2000plus","firmware_ver":"1.2","catid":"X"}

Parameters:

  • mode = movies_list (will list all available Movies in database.)
  • code , mac , sn, chipid, model, firmware_ver: already mentioned.
  • catid = the category id (sub_id) from previous step.
  • note: to get all movies list set catid=all.

Step 3 : Movie Info (Details)

Get Movie Info (Details).
http://gean4563t.xyz:80/V6/API-V6.php
json: {"mode":"movies_info","code":"111111111111","mac":"00:17:B3:EB:A0:59","sn":"0017.B3EB.A059","chipid":"123","model":"2000plus","firmware_ver":"1.2","movie_id":"138468"}

Parameters:

  • mode = movies_info
  • movie_id = 138468 (id as got from previous step)
  • code , mac , sn, chipid, model, firmware_ver: already mentioned.

Returned Value: JSON

 [
    {
        "id": "138468",
        "stream_display_name": "Sergio 2020",
        "category_id": 192,
        "stream_icon": "https://image.tmdb.org/t/p/w600_and_h900_bestv2/tABtj9BwYVDCM69h7AK38MDgw64.jpg",

        "movie_image": "https://image.tmdb.org/t/p/w600_and_h900_bestv2/tABtj9BwYVDCM69h7AK38MDgw64.jpg",

        "plot": "Au milieu du chaos causé par l'invasion américaine en Irak, Sergio Vieira de Mello, un haut diplomate des Nations Unies, risque sa vie lors de la mission la plus périlleuse de sa carrière.",

Get Top Series List

Get List of Series for certain categories.
http://gean4563t.xyz:80/V6/API-V6.php
json: {"mode":"series_list","code":"111111111111","mac":"00:17:B3:EB:A0:59","sn":"0017.B3EB.A059","chipid":"123","model":"2000plus","firmware_ver":"1.2","catid":"X"}

Get Top Series Details ( season with episodes )

to get Series info, you must set series_id value 
json: {"mode":"series_info","code":"111111111111","mac":"00:17:B3:EB:A0:59","sn":"0017.B3EB.A059","chipid":"123","model":"2000plus","firmware_ver":"1.2","series_id":"{series_id}"}

Parameters:

  • mode = series_info
  • series_id = {series_id}
  • code , mac , sn, chipid, model, firmware_ver: already mentioned.
